TOUR-P01    PASS    the tour follows the PERSON across three independent sessions -- each one a fresh set of JWT claims, which is what a logout, another browser and another machine all are from here. Skipping is not a call, so a skipped tour is offered again; exactly THREE automatic presentations are given and the fourth is refused by the declared limit; a fourth call is a no-op and does not move the count. Dismissal stops automatic presentation, completion stops it, completing twice keeps the first instant, and a completed tour spends no presentation. No device-local value is written or read at any point: every answer comes from the database. Every row rolled back
